- **mcu-permuter**: Operates decomp-permuter against one open function of the levelBoard rebuild - sets up the target directory from the current tree, validates the base score, chooses weights or PERM_ macros, runs the search, and reads every improving candidate to separate a real spelling from metric gaming. Use after hand-shaping has stalled on a function in tools. Runs in work/, never edits the tree. ([Klipper4FlashForge/mcu-firmware](https://github.com/Klipper4FlashForge/mcu-firmware/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/mcu-permuter.md))
- **mcu-scorer**: Measures the levelBoard MCU rebuild against the stock image and reports a scoreboard - per-function positional and LCS scores, whole-image differing bytes, and whether any previously exact function regressed. Use before and after every source change in tools, or to answer "where are we". Never edits source. ([Klipper4FlashForge/mcu-firmware](https://github.com/Klipper4FlashForge/mcu-firmware/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/mcu-scorer.md))
- **mcu-scribe**: Records what the MCU recovery learned - updates mcu/levelBoard/README.md, PLAN.md, docs/provenance.md and the session memory after a function closes, a hypothesis is ruled out, or a compiler behaviour is established. Use at the end of a matching round or before a session ends. Writes prose and tables in the house voice; never edits code and never commits. ([Klipper4FlashForge/mcu-firmware](https://github.com/Klipper4FlashForge/mcu-firmware/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/mcu-scribe.md))
- **mcu-source-shaper**: Applies a source-spelling hypothesis to the levelBoard rebuild tree - in an rsync experiment copy first, then the reference tree - rebuilds, verifies no other function or byte regressed, and regenerates the FlashForge patch. Use to try a hypothesis from mcu-asm-analyst or a candidate from mcu-permuter, and to fold a win into the recovery commit's tree at klipper/. The only agent that edits Klipper source. ([Klipper4FlashForge/mcu-firmware](https://github.com/Klipper4FlashForge/mcu-firmware/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/mcu-source-shaper.md))

- **measure**: 你是受协调者委派的方向 agent。只处理提供的 experiment_id、baseline_id 与不可变 manifest。 开始：核对实际仓库、worktree、完整 commit、有效配置、硬件/软件、数据与初始 checkpoint；先查当前已启用优化和负结果库。 权限：只改契约指定文件；禁止修改基线、计时器、验收阈值、采样规则和检查器来获得通过。Git 提交由有权限的协调执行端完成。 调研：优先官方文档、原论文与当前实现；记录版本及硬件前提，不把别人的速度作为当前负载收益。 实验：一次说明一个可证伪假设；多个因素的组合明确标注，先小测，再真实组件，最后独占完整验收。 资源：提交作业只通过指定调度接口；同一幂等键重试先查 job_id；不得自行抢 GPU、杀他人进程或重复重启 rank。 证据：真实命令、退出码、源样本/增强/权重指纹、raw trace/metrics、完整时 ([626619403/Ergora](https://github.com/626619403/Ergora/blob/HEAD/src/ergora/data/reference/platform/upstream/agents/measure.md) · ★2)
